*GPT message: It seems the app did not crash as expected. Instead, after I clicked "Download current view", it returned to the main map screen. The bug report is very specific that a crash should occur. The "quickly disappearing UI" might have been the download process starting and then failing silently, but not with a fatal exception that closes the app.

There are a few possibilities:
1.  The bug is not reproducible on this version or device configuration.
2.  A crucial precondition was not met. The most likely candidate is the "Max. age" setting. I was unable to save it as "0" and had to proceed with "365". The bug might only trigger when the age is exactly "0".
3.  The download itself failed for another reason (e.g., network issue, API limit), which prevented the buggy code path from being reached.

Given that I was stuck in a loop trying to set the age to "0", I will try a different approach to set up the validator preferences correctly. *GPT message: I have now tried multiple times to trigger the crash by downloading the map data, even after zooming into a very small area as suggested by the bug report.
